Another angle: Use **randomized/Kac** or **simulated annealing** on p with local swaps. The objective is max of many quadratics. Coordinate descent exact can polish. We can combine:
1. Generate diverse initial sequences from continuous theories and random.
2. Use global spectral/mirror descent to get near flat p (fast, many iterations).

Let's design exact coordinate descent on simplex (sum fixed=1) with O(n) per coordinate and fast minimization of convex 1D objective. How to minimize exactly O(1)? AO LP step: Given p normalized, solve q = argmin_{q≥0,∑q=1} max_k (p*q)_k. We can approximate via **exponential multiplicative weights for the min-max game**:
Initialize q=p (or uniform). For t in T:
  v = p*q (length 2n-1) via FFT.
  y = softmax(λ*v) (weights on high constraints)
  h = A^T y via FFT (length n)
  q = softmax(-μ*h + small entropy?) (i.e., q ∝ exp(-μ h))
This is the mirror descent dual of the game. At convergence, q's such that p*q balanced.
Then set p ← (1-λ_p)p + λ_p q. This resembles previous but much faster than LP.
Could also use this to optimize p directly: The fixed point of q update and p update might reduce max p*p.

Let's formulate game value for q: min_q max_k (p*q)_k. If p=q at fixed point, value=max p*p. The dual y are weights on active lags. We can solve q update to minimize max(p*q), and then average with p. We can tune λ_p and number of inner iterations. This may be more principled than subgradient.

This resembles generating a probability distribution on integers (sums of independent variables) with bounded probability mass. If p is probability on 0..n-1. Then p*p is distribution of sum of two independent copies. We want bound max probability. This is discrete: choose distribution on {0,...,n-1} to make sum of two iid not too concentrated. This is exactly a combinatorial additive combinatorics problem. The best is to make distribution like a random variable on a cyclic group with convolution as flat as possible. 3. Main optimization cores:
   a. **Mirror descent (z/logit) with soft weights**:
      ```
      def mirror_descent(p, iterations, eta, beta, ...):
         z = log(p + eps) - max(logp); p=softmax(z)
         for it:
            c = conv(p,p); cm = max(c)
            L = cm + logsumexp(beta*(c-cm))/beta
            w = exp(beta*(c-cm)); w/=sum
            h = conv_weights_with_p(w, p)  # length n
            # h_i maybe 2*Σ w_k p_{k-i}; ensure nonneg
            # normalize h to mean 1? h_mean = n^2? Let's scale: h_i = n * 2 * sum w p. For p~1/n, w~active length, h~ O(1)
            # update z -= alpha * (h - h.mean())
            # optional mix p with uniform to avoid collapse
         ```

   b. **AO min-max game for q**:
      ```
      def ao_update(p, inner_iters, ...):
         q = p.copy()
         for _ in range(inner):
             v = conv(p,q)
             # normalize? sum v =1
             T = ...; w = softmax(v/T - max) # weights on high
             h = cross(w,p)  # length n, h_j = sum w_k p_{k-j}
             # update q via softmax(-eta*h)
             h_rel = n*h; zq = log(q)+? - eta*(h_rel-mean)
             q = softmax(zq)
         return (1-lambda)*p + lambda*q
      ```
      This directly solves one AO step. We can alternate p update with small λ (e.g., 0.2) to avoid oscillation. This may be powerful.

Now, optimization core details:

### 1. Smoothed LSE L-BFGS in logit space
Function `lse_score_grad_z(z, beta, n)`:
- z = z - z.mean() to center.
- p = exp(z); p /= p.sum(). (If z range huge, exp overflow. We can clip z to [-50,50]? But softmax needs relative. Use q = z - max; p=exp(q); p/=sum. Values could underflow to zero. We can add small floor: p = p + eps; p /= sum? This changes objective. Maybe set z within [-40,40] by clipping; but optimization may need zeros. We can allow some zeros. For L-BFGS, zeros create non-differentiability? Softmax supports zeros if exp underflows; gradient zero there. Could be okay. To maintain positivity, after p compute p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300). Normalize.
- c = conv(p,p). maxc = c.max(). scaled = beta*(c-maxc). If beta large, exp underflow. We can set beta based on c range: `T = max(1e-18, c.max() - c.min())`; beta = 1/(gamma*T), gamma 0.05..1. But for L-BFGS, beta fixed maybe. Use adaptive: beta = 1/(0.1*(maxc - mean?)) etc.
- LSE = maxc + log(sum(exp(scaled)))/beta.
- score = 2n*LSE / S^2. If S=1, 2n LSE.
- p weights w = exp(scaled); w /= sum.
- h = cross_weights_p(w, p). This is Σ w_k p_{k-j}. The gradient of LSE wrt p: dLSE/dp_i = 2 h_i. Wait derivative of c_k = 2Σ w_k p_{k-i}? For LSE = maxc + (1/β) log Σ exp(β c_k). Gradient = (1/|?|) Σ w_k ∇c_k. ∇c_k_i = 2 p_{k-i} (with missing out=0). So ∇LSE = 2 Σ w_k p_{k-i} = 2h_i.
- score wrt p = factor * ∇LSE - factor * (2*S? if S not fixed). If S=1 exactly, no denominator gradient. But due p not exactly sum1? We'll normalize; approximate. For logit z, p=softmax. ### 2. Mirror/AO heuristics
Mirror descent in z/logit (full n) maybe simpler and can run many iterations cheaply. It can also produce candidate to feed L-BFGS.
Algorithm `smoothed_mirror(p, iterations, beta_schedule, alpha_schedule)`:
- z = log(p + 1e-300); z -= max(z). (Scale invariant). p=softmax(z). We don't need fixed last.
- For each iter:
   c=conv(z->p,p), maybe compute p each iteration.
   maxc=c.max(); range=maxc - c.min(); T=max(1e-18, temp_range*range). beta=1/T.
   scaled=beta*(c-maxc); w=exp(scaled); w/=sum.
   h=cross(w,p) # length n; h_i=Σw_k p_{k-i}
   h_rel = n*h # O(1)
   # Maybe subtract mean to preserve scale: d = h_rel - h_rel.mean(); d /= max(1e-12, np.std(d)+1e-12)? If std small, no update.
   z -= alpha * d * step_scale? Actually ∇_z ≈ p*(?); using log update approximates mirror descent on p, not exact gradient. We can set update = - alpha * (h_rel - mean). This changes p multiplicatively: p_new ∝ p exp(-α(h_rel-mean)). Good.
   # boundary: p can become very uneven. If max(p) > max_ratio*avg (e.g., 50/n? 100/n), blend with uniform or add floor.
  Keep best exact score when p changes.

But h_rel may be very noisy; use exponential moving average of d? Use momentum: v = μ v + d; z -= lr * v. lr small (e.g., 0.01-0.1). Since n~8000, h_rel values O(0-5). lr 0.05 maybe.

We need calibrate. If p uniform, c triangular, w for low temp center. h_rel for uniform? Let's approximate h_rel_i = n*2Σ_center weights p*k? For uniform p=1/n, h_rel_i=2*weight_count. If weights count ~active length L_w. h_rel maybe 2L_w. If L_w ~100, update d up to 200, lr 0.01 => z change 2, maybe okay. We can normalize d by its max or std. Use `d = h_rel - h_rel.mean(); d = d / max(np.max(np.abs(d)),1e-12)` to make step scale α. Then α in [0.05,2]. Good. This update changes p multiplicatively exp(-α*d_normalized). If α=0.2, small. We'll sweep α.

AO update q:
```
def ao_step(p, inner=10, temp=0.1, eta=1.0):
   q = p.copy()
   zq = log(q); zq -= max
   Pfft = fft(p); Preffft = fft(p[::-1])
   for it:
      Qfft = fft(q); v=ifft(Pfft*Qfft)[:2n-1]; v=np.real; v=np.maximum(v,0); S=1? sum v=1.
      maxv=v.max(); T=temp*max(1e-18, maxv-v.min()); w=exp((v-maxv)/T); w/=sum
      Wfft=fft(w, M2)
      h=ifft(Preffft*Wfft, M2)[n-1:2n-1]
      h_rel=n*h; d=h_rel - mean; d/=max(abs)
      zq -= eta*d
      q=softmax(zq)
      occasional recenter zq? subtract mean? Since scale invariance but p fixed; q sum normalized. zq -= mean each iter to keep bounded.
   p_new = (1-lam)p + lam*q
   normalize
```
But Pfft length M=next_pow2(2n-1) for v; Preffft length M2 for h. p fixed. q update uses zq. This solves min max(p*q). We can run inner 5-50. Then p update λ 0.2-0.5. This can be repeated.

This AO might be more principled and faster than coordinate. We can use it as a main search. Need be careful: q computed to balance p*q, but next p*p update. Maybe analogous to regularized iterative hard thresholding.

Let's analyze AO game more to ensure it can improve. Suppose p is current. q=min max(p*q). If p is uniform, what is q? p uniform 1/n. (p*q)_k = (1/n) Σ_{j: k-j in [0,n-1]} q_j. Let's implement AO with robust parameters:
```
def ao_improve(p, time_limit, outer=1000, inner=8, lam=0.2, temp=0.1):
   p = normalize
   for outer in time:
      Q = p.copy(); zq=log(Q+eps); center
      for inner:
         v=conv(p,Q) (real, clip small neg)
         # value v sum 1
         vmax=v.max(); vmin=v.min(); T=temp*max(1e-18, vmax-vmin)
         w=exp((v-vmax)/T); w/=sum
         h=cross(w,p)  # O(n log n)
         h_rel=n*h; d=h_rel - h_rel.mean(); denom=max(np.max(abs(d)),1e-12); d/=denom
         zq -= eta * d # eta 0.1-1.0
         zq -= zq.mean()
         Q=softmax(zq)
         # optional mix Q with uniform? p of Q?
      qm = Q
      p = (1-lam)*p + lam*qm
      p = normalize
      score exact maybe every few outers; update best
```
But computing v=conv(p,Q) and h=cross(w,p) each inner: 3 FFTs? v one (Pfft*Qfft), h one (Preffft*Wfft) plus Qfft and Wfft. p fixed Pfft/Preffft. That's 4 FFTs/inv. If inner=8, outer=1000 =>8000*4*FFT maybe large but okay? n=8192 FFT 16384 ~0.05ms? Actually Numpy FFT 16k ~0.2ms? 32k for h ~0.3ms, total maybe 1ms per inner; 8000 ~8s? Maybe. Python overhead more. Good.

We can reduce by inner=2-5. We can also update Q using active high weights from v without W FFT? Need h. Could compute h by direct for sparse w if w concentrated. For high temp, w broad; FFT needed. Use adaptive: if weight effective support small (sum w^2 > threshold), use direct loops over nonzero w above threshold. But keep simple.

Choose eta: If d normalized, eta around 0.2-1. We can line search for outer p update: after q computed, try p_new = (1-lam)p+lam q for lam in [0.1,0.3,0.5], compute score (conv) choose best? That adds conv cost. Maybe just fix lam=0.3 and use best tracking; if score worsens, p still maybe beneficial? To avoid恶化, we can keep current `p_cur` and only accept p_new if exact score (or estimated) improves or not too bad. But AO may need temporary worsening to escape. We can keep best separately, and allow p_cur to wander. Use simulated annealing: accept if score < cur_score + tol.

Let's detail random smooth seed:
```
def random_smooth_p(n, rng, fmax=8):
   w = np.zeros(n)
   # real symmetric FFT
   r = rng.standard_normal(n//2+1)
   # damp high freq: r *= exp(- (freq/fmax)**2)
   freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
   damp = np.exp(-(freq/fmax)**2)
   f = np.fft.irfft(r*damp, n)
   p = np.exp(f - f.max()) # positive
   p = p + rng.random(n)*0.01? clip
   p = p / p.sum()
```
This may produce periodic wiggles. Also can use random low-pass in time: smooth white noise via cumsum? Fourier good.

Cosine seeds:
```
def cosine_seed(n, m, alpha, phase=0):
   x=np.arange(n)/(n-1)
   p = 1 + alpha*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + phase)
   p=np.clip(p,1e-12,None); p/=p.sum()
```
Use odd frequency with phase to anti-center: `p = 1 + alpha*cos((2*m+1)*np.pi*x)`? Since x∈[0,1], at x=1 cos(oddπ)=-1. Nonnegative if alpha≤1. Include m 1,3,7.

Endpoint seeds: p_i = 1+N*smooth? random.

### L-BFGS implementation
Need avoid closures/lambdas. Define top-level `minimize_lse_z(p, beta_param, maxfun, deadline?)` but minimize cannot take deadline. We'll call with maxfun limited by time estimate.
We can implement our own simple **L-BFGS via scipy**. Since no closures, function `lse_objective_z(x, beta, n)` returns f,g. It will build z. But `scipy.optimize.minimize` passes `args`, okay. Could call multiple times with smaller maxfun and check time.

However, objective in z with softmax and LSE has scale invariance; setting last z=0 breaks scale but if p initialized, the log ratios determine p. The absolute scale of z doesn't matter, but L-BFGS can move absolute scale due last fixed? It may wander, causing p last change. Fine. To keep p_last not too small, we can periodically re-center? If we don't center, adding a constant to all except last changes p? Softmax is invariant to adding constant to all z including last; since last fixed, the optimizer might use the scale degree to smoothly change all values. That is allowed and affects p. Not an issue.

But if z values become large, exp under/overflow. In softmax, do `z = x; z[-1]=0; z = z - np.max(z); p=exp(z); p/=p.sum()`. This subtracts max; if max large, relative okay. Gradient mapping uses p. If some p underflow zero, OK.

Function:
```
def lse_z_objective(x, beta, n):
    z = np.empty(n); z[:-1]=x; z[-1]=0.0
    z -= z.max()
    p = np.exp(z); p_sum = p.sum()
    if p_sum <=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    p /= p_sum
    c = conv_self(p) # maybe use cache? no
    cm = c.max()
    scaled = beta*(c-cm)
    # prevent overflow: if beta large, scaled min very negative; exp okay if >-745.
    ew = np.exp(scaled)
    sumw = ew.sum()
    if not finite: ... maybe set beta fx
    w = ew/sumw
    L = cm + np.log(sumw)/beta
    score = 2.0*n*L  # p normalized
    h = cross_weights_p(w,p)  # length n
    grad_p = 2.0*h
    # Because p normalized, no sum term
    Sg = np.dot(p, grad_p)
    gz = p * (grad_p - Sg*p)
    # For x gradient
    gx = gz[:-1]
    # scale gradient for numeric: gx /= n? Since L-BFGS uses magnitude. Values c~1e-4, grad_p~1e-4? Actually h~1/n? If n=8000, h~1e-4; grad_p~2e-4; p~1e-4; gz~p*(grad) ~2e-8. Very small. L-BFGS might think converged. We can scale gradient by large factor e.g., 1e6 or use score times? Multiply gx by 1e5/n? Let's compute: p~1/n, grad_p~1/n, p*grad~1/n^2~1e-8. L-BFGS tolerances default 1e-8, might stop. We can scale objective and gradient by `scale = 1e6` or use `return score, gx*1e6`. Score ~1.5. Gradient scaled accordingly. Choose scale=1e6/n? For n=8000 scale=125000 gives grad ~0.00125. Good. We can multiply by `grad_scale = 1e6 / max(1,n)`.
```
But if p normalized, score not sensitive to scale? p sum=1 due softmax, no scale invariance after last fixed. Good.

We need choose beta. If beta is absolute, c~1e-4; beta=1e5 gives scaled range. For L-BFGS, use beta values [1e4,1e5,3e5,1e6] maybe. Since c max ~1e-4, beta*c ~10. Could define `beta = round_target / (cm - c_mean?)` but objective requires fixed beta. We can estimate initial beta = 1/(0.05 * max(1e-12, np.max(c)-np.min(c))) from p. If range ~1e-5, beta~200000. Use list around.

L-BFGS options: `ftol=1e-12, gtol=1e-6` maybe scaled gradient. `maxiter=200, maxfun=5000`. We can call with `maxfun` 2000. It may not improve. To avoid long, run in chunks.

But **scipy minimize with n=8000 variable** may use dense memory? L-BFGS-B memory 20 * 8000 *? okay. It uses line search computing f/g at multiple points. Could be okay.

Let's implement exact `score_normalized(p)` maybe c=conv, max, score=2n*max (sum assumed 1). For internal and best. To avoid repeated sum, normalize p before.

Functions:
```
def fft_len(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def conv_self(a):
   n=len(a); m=2*n-1; M=fft_len(m); A=np.fft.rfft(a, M); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:m]; return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
Clipping negative to 0 may hide errors but nonnegative sequence => conv nonnegative. FFT small negatives possible; clipping good. But if truly small positive clipped? okay. Sum clipped slightly changes; for score max only. For CD update, if c clipped, drift may change sum? We recompute.
`cross_weights_p(w,a)` as above, clip small negative? w nonneg, a nonneg => h nonneg; FFT negatives small clip.

`normalize_p(p, in_place=False)`:
```
p = np.asarray(p,float); p[p<0]=0; s=p.sum(); if s<1e-12: return ones/n; if not in_place: p=p/s; return p
```

`make_resample(parent, n)`:
```
if len(parent)==n: p=parent.copy()
else: x_old=np.arange(len(parent)); x_new=np.linspace(0,len(parent)-1,n); p=np.interp(x_new, x_old, parent)
return normalize_p(p)
```

`random_smooth_p(n, rng, max_freq=None)`:
```
if max_freq is None: max_freq = 5.0
freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
amp=np.exp(-(freq/max_freq)**2)
r=rng.standard_normal(n//2+1)*amp
f=np.fft.irfft(r,n)
f = f - f.max()
# avoid too sharp by exp scaling
p=np.exp(0.5*f) # moderate
p = p + 1e-8
return normalize_p(p)
```
Could produce near uniform if noise low. Add alpha.

`cosine_p(n, m, alpha, phase=0)`:
```
x=np.arange(n)/(n-1)
p=1+alpha*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + phase)
p=np.clip(p,1e-12,None)
return normalize_p(p)
```
Use odd `2πm+?`? Include `odd=True` with cos((2m+1)π x) to anti-center.

### AO optimize details in code
```
def ao_optimize(p, budget_s, rng, lam=0.3, inner=4, eta=0.3, temp=0.08, max_outers=None, min_improve=1e-9):
    p=normalize_p(p)
    n=len(p); M=fft_len(2*n-1); M2=fft_len(3*n-2)
    Pfft=np.fft.rfft(p,M); Prfft=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],M2)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_norm(p)
    outers=0; no_improve=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < t0+budget_s:
       Q=p.copy()
       zq=np.log(Q+1e-300); zq-=zq.max()
       # Initialize Q maybe mix with perturbation: if no_improve high, perturb zq with noise
       for _ in range(inner):
           Qf=np.fft.rfft(Q,M)
           v=np.fft.irfft(Pfft*Qf,M)[:2*n-1].real
           v=np.maximum(v,0); s=v.sum(); if s>0: v/=s
           vmax=v.max(); vmin=v.min(); rng0=max(1e-18, vmax-vmin)
           T=temp* rng0
           # if T too small, use percentiles: maybe T=0.05*range
           w=np.exp((v-vmax)/T); sumw=w.sum(); if not finite or sumw==0: w=np.zeros; w[int(argmax)]=1 else w/=sumw
           Wf=np.fft.rfft(w, M2)
           h=np.fft.irfft(Prfft*Wf, M2)[n-1:2*n-1].real
           h=np.maximum(h,0)
           hrel=n*h
           d=hrel - hrel.mean()
           dmax=np.max(np.abs(d))
           if dmax>1e-14:
               zq = zq - (eta/dmax)*d? 
```

```
def eval_t_exact(t, i, c, p, x, S):
   S_new=S-x+t
```

```
def hot_order(n,p,top_idx):
   # use first top lag to sort contributors
   k=int(top_idx[0])
   j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
   d=np.zeros(n); d[j0:j1+1]=p[j0:j1+1][::-1]
   return np.argsort(d)[::-1]
```

### L-BFGS and positivity
In `lse_z_objective`, if p has zeros, gradient zero for zero coords. L-BFGS x initialized with log(p_floor). It can increase zeros if gradient. Underflow: z range maybe >700. p=exp(z-max); if max-z>745, zero. OK. Sumw may underflow if beta*cmax? scale shifted by maxc, max scaled 0, sumw≥1. Good.
`if not np.isfinite(LSE): return 1e100, zeros`.
Gradient scale: `grad_scale = 1e6 / max(1,n)` passed. In objective: `return score, gx * grad_scale`.
If score nonfinite, gradient zeros.
`gz = p * (grad_p - dot)`. If p has zeros, okay. `gx = gz[:-1]`.
But if x length n-1, and n=1? n at least 64. For n=1, L-BFGS variable 0; not needed.

`minimize` may call with x containing inf/nan? We can sanitize at start? If res.x nonfinite, fallback. In objective, if nonfinite return large.

Line search may evaluate x large causing z max huge; stable.

### AO weights and h normalization
In `ao_optimize`, after computing v, sum may be zero? v nonnegative, sum ~1. If v all zero (p zero), handle.
`T = temp * max(1e-18, vmax - vmin)`. If range=0, T=1e-18, exp((v-vmax)/T) -> all 1? if v-vmax=0, yes; if some negative small? We clipped v nonneg. Good.
`wsum = w.sum(); if not finite or wsum<=0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1`. `argmax = int(np.argmax(v))`.
`h = cross_weights_p(w, p)` uses p old. If p sum 1, okay.
`hrel = n*h`. d normalization: `dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))`; if dmax<1e-14 continue.
`zq -= eta * (d/dmax)`. Eta perhaps 0.5. To avoid zq drift, after inner `zq -= zq.mean(); zq -= zq.max()`. If zq all -inf due underflow? We maintain zq from real values.
`Q = np.exp(zq); Q /= Q.sum()`. If sum not finite, Q=p.
`p_new = (1-lam)*p + lam*Q`. Normalize.
Score: `sc=score_norm(p_new)` uses conv. We can compute every outer. If sc<best: update. If no improve 20 outers, perturb p:
```

```
Also adaptive lam: if improve, lam = min(0.6, lam*1.05); if no improve, lam = max(0.05, lam*0.9).

```
def make_seeds(parent,N,rng):
   seeds=[]
   if parent is not None:
      p=resample_sequence(parent,N); seeds.append(p)
      # noisy parent
      for st in [0.05,0.2]:
         seeds.append(perturb_p(p,rng,strength=st,mode=0))
   seeds.append(np.ones(N)/N)
   # cosines
   for m in [1,2,3,5,7,11]:
      for alpha in [0.3,0.7,1.0]:
         for phase in [0.0, 0.5*np.pi]:
            p=cosine_seed(N,m,alpha,phase,odd=(m%2==0?))
   # random smooth
   for _ in range(4): seeds.append(random_smooth_p(N,rng,...))
   # maybe endpoint-separating: p_i = 1+alpha*(2i/N-1) clipped (linear ramp)
```

### `optimize_length` more concrete
```
def optimize_length(p, budget_s, rng, label=''):
    if budget_s <= 5: return p, score_norm(p)
    p = normalize_p(p)
    best_p = p.copy(); best_sc = score_norm(p)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    def within(t): ... cannot nested. We'll use inline time checks.

    # AO1
    if t0+budget_s-30 > time...:
        ao_budget = min(420.0, 0.30*budget_s)
        p, best_p, best_sc = ao_optimize(p, ao_budget, rng, label)
    # LSE chunk
    rem = (t0+budget_s) - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60:
       betas = estimate_betas(best_p)
       # maybe use only first 3 betas
       for idx,beta in enumerate(betas[:3]):
          rem = ...
          if rem < 30: break
          maxfun = 1200 if len(best_p)>10000 else 2500
          try res...
          p_new, sc = run_lse(best_p, beta, maxfun)
          if sc < best_sc: best...
          # cd polish
          if time...:
             pc=best_p.copy(); cc=conv_self(pc)
             for sw in range(2): if time: pc,cc,sc=active_cd_sweep(... top_count=80, order='hot') ; if sc<best: update
    # AO2
    rem = ...
    if rem > 90:
       ao_budget = min(360, 0.25*rem)
       p, best_p, best_sc = ao_optimize(best_p, ao_budget, rng, label)
    # CD
    rem = ...
    if rem > 60:
       p_cur=best_p.copy(); c_cur=conv_self(p_cur); sc_cur=score_norm(p_cur)
       top_count=100 if n<10000 else 60
       no_impr=0
       while rem > 30:
          order = 'hot' if (sweep_count%3==0) else 'random'
          p_cur,c_cur,sc_cur=active_cd_sweep(p_cur,c_cur,top_count,order,rng)
          if sc_cur < best_sc: best... no_impr=0
          elif sc_cur < best_sc* (1+1e-5): no_impr +=1
          else: p_cur=best_p.copy(); c_cur=conv_self(p_cur); sc_cur=best_sc; no_impr += 2
          if no_impr >=6:
              p_cur = perturb_p(best_p, rng, strength=0.1+0.2*rng.random())
              c_cur=conv_self(p_cur); sc_cur=score_norm(p_cur)
              no_impr=0
    # final small LSE
    rem=...
    if rem > 40: run one beta from estimate with small maxfun and CD once
    return best_p,best_sc
```
We cannot use nested `within`, so compute `remaining = t_deadline - time.monotonic()` each time.

`ao_optimize` returns best_p and best_sc. It might modify p local. Need if it doesn't improve, return initial best. Good.

`run_lse` top-level:
```
def run_lse(p,beta,maxfun=2000):
    p=normalize_p(p)
    n=len(p)
    pf=p+1e-300; pf/=pf.sum()
    x=np.log(pf)-np.log(pf[-1])  # if pf[-1] zero use min
    # If pf[-1] very small, log ratio large; okay if finite.
    z = np.empty(n); z[:-1]=x; z[-1]=0; z-=z.max(); p0=exp(z); # p0 should approx pf
    grad_scale=1e6/max(1,n)
    res=minimize(lse_z_objective, x, args=(beta,n,grad_scale), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=...)
    x_new=res.x if finite else x
    z=...
    p_new=normalize_p(exp(z))
    sc=score_norm(p_new)
    if nonfinite: return p, score_norm(p)
    return p_new, sc
```
If `pf[-1]` zero, log(min). Use `den = max(float(pf[-1]), 1e-300)`. x=log(pf)-log(den). If some pf zero, log(1e-300) finite.
Option: `maxiter` maybe int(maxfun/5)? Use 300.
If minimize fails, still res.x? It may set x. For safety, `res` has x.

### Mirror vs AO: Is AO solving the right q? The q update with zq -= eta*(h_rel-mean). h_rel = n * A^T w, where w weights high p*q. This is mirror descent for min max(p*q)? Let's check sign: We want q to minimize max (P q). Subgradient w.r.t q of soft max is P^T w. Mirror descent on simplex: q_j ∝ exp(-η (P^T w)_j). We use h_rel = n*(P^T w)_j, which is O(1). So decreasing high h_j. Good. The dual update for w? We set w=softmax(v/T) where v=P q. This is dual ascent. This is standard. Good.
The temperature T relative to v range. To make q more aggressive, use low T; to smooth, high. We can maybe adapt T = 0.15*(vmax - median). We use min. For near flat, range small; weights broad. Good.

### Check objective factor in optimize best
`score_norm(p)` returns `2.0*n*cmax` if p sum normalized. If p not exactly sum1, we can compute exact to be safe:
```
def score_normalized(p):
   s=float(p.sum())
   if s<1e-12: return inf
   c=conv_self(p)
   return 2.0*len(p)*float(c.max())/(s*s)
```
Use this for all, not assume sum1. In AO p normalized but sum may drift slightly. Active CD before normalize not score. L-BFGS p normalized. So use exact.

For all active t likely all t if flat max. Condition: For all x,y? Maybe f satisfies f(x)+f(t-x) constant for active t? = t + a∫(cosπx+cosπ(t-x)) + a^2∫ cosπx cosπ(t-x).

### Active CD exactness with sum normalization

Random polynomial seed:
```
def polynomial_seed(n, rng, degree=3, max_coeff=1.5):
    x=np.linspace(-0.5,0.5,n)
    p=1.0
    for k in range(1,degree+1):
        c=rng.uniform(-max_coeff,max_coeff)
        p += c * (x**k)
    p=np.clip(p,1e-8,None); normalize
```
This could be diverse. Clip to positive.

Direct mirror:
```
for iter:
 c=conv(p,p); w=softmax(βc); h=cross(w,p); d=n*h - mean; z -= α d/maxd; p=softmax(z)
```
This is one conv + cross per iter (2 FFT) vs AO inner 3-4. Could be main search. Let's add `mirror_optimize` and run before/after AO. It may escape local and is cheap.
Function `mirror_optimize(p,budget_s,rng, alpha=0.2, temp=0.1)` similar to AO but no Q inner. It can line search? Just track best. Steps many.
Pseudo:
```
z=log(p+eps); center
while time:
  p=softmax(z)
  c=conv_self(p)
  vmax=vmax; w=softmax(c, T)
  h=cross(w,p)
  d=n*h; d-=mean; dmax...
  z -= alpha*(d/dmax)
  z -= z.mean(); z-=z.max()
  optional compensate polarity: if max(p)>... blend
  eval score every few
```
This gradient is not AO but descent of LSE. Since objective nonconvex, may help. Add to optimize_length as Phase 0 or after AO. Budget 20%.

```

```

`random_smooth_p`:
```
def random_smooth_p(n,rng,alpha=0.8,fmax=3.0):
    freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    damp=np.exp(-(freqs/fmax)**2)
    coeff=rng.standard_normal(n//2+1)*damp
    f=np.fft.irfft(coeff,n)
    # standardize
    sd=f.std()
    if sd>1e-12: f=alpha*f/sd
    # maybe add a random mean? irrelevant
    p=np.exp(f)
    p=np.clip(p,1e-12,None)
    return normalize_p(p)
```
Alpha 0.5-1.5.

`polynomial_seed`: as above. `cosine_seed` with odd option.

`block_seed`: `B = min(n, int(block))`; weights = rng.exponential(size=B); p = np.repeat(weights, n//B + ...); last adjust; normalize.

#### Implementation details for `active_cd_sweep`
Inputs: `p` normalized? Could be any, but objective uses denominator S. Let's define `exact=True`? Use current S. If p not normalized, active objective uses S. At end normalize. Good.
```
def active_cd_sweep(p, c, top_count=120, order='random', rng=None):
    n=len(p)
    p=p.copy()
    c=c.copy()
    S=float(p.sum())
    L=2*n-1
    top_count=min(top_count,L)
    pref,suff,top_idx = build_aux(c,top_count)
    if order == 'hot' and top_idx.size:
       k=int(top_idx[0])
       d=np.zeros(n)
       j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
       d[j0:j1+1]=p[j0:j1+1][::-1]
       order_arr=np.argsort(d)[::-1]
    elif order=='seq': order_arr=np.arange(n)
    else: order_arr=np.arange(n) if rng is None else rng.permutation(n)
    updates=0
    for i in order_arr:
       i=int(i)
       x=float(p[i])
       # active vals
       ks=top_idx
       aff=(ks>=i)&(ks<i+n)
       out_max = 0.0
       if i>0 and pref[i-1]>out_max: out_max=float(pref[i-1])
       endk=i+n
       if endk<L and suff[endk]>out_max: out_max=float(suff[endk])
       # monitored outside
       if ks.size:
          outside_mask=~aff
          if np.any(outside_mask):
             om=float(np.max(c[ks[outside_mask]]))
             if om>out_max: out_max=om
          if np.any(aff) and c[ks[aff]].max()>out_max: pass # included in base later
       S_rest=S-x
       if S_rest < -1e-12: ... but p nonneg.
       hi=float(np.clip(max(0.005, 3.0*x+1.0/n, 50.0/n), 0.0, 1.0))
       # if x==1, hi maybe >? set hi = min(hi, 0.999)
       if hi <= x + 1e-12 and x > 1e-12: hi = min(1.0, max(hi, x*1.05))
       if hi <= 0: hi=1e-6
       # base arrays if aff
       if np.any(aff):
          ks_aff=ks[aff]
          j=ks_aff-i
          old=c[ks_aff]
          p_aff=p[j]
          diag=(j==i)
          base=old - 2.0*x*p_aff
          if np.any(diag): base[diag] += x*x
          A=p_aff.copy(); if np.any(diag): A[diag]=0.0
          diagf=diag.astype(float)
       else:
          base=np.empty(0); A=base; diagf=base
       # current active obj
       beobj=active_coord_obj(x,S_rest,out_max,base,A,diagf)
       # golden
       lo=0.0; hbound=hi
       # if x not in [lo,hi], maybe set beobj? if x>hi, shrink? ensure hi>=x? For monotonic? We can if x>hi: hi=x*1.2; lo=0.
       if hi < x: hi = max(hi, x)
       # sample endpoints
       f0=active_coord_obj(0.0,...)
       if f0<beobj: beobj=f0; beidx=0
       fhi=active_coord_obj(hi,...)
       if fhi<beobj: beobj=fhi; beidx=1
       # if beidx not set initialize beot
       ...
       # if hi>lo
       u=lo + (1-gr)*(hi-lo); v=lo+gr*(hi-lo)
       fu=active_coord_obj(u,...); fv=active_coord_obj(v,...)
       for _ in range(20): ...
       topt=(lo+hi)/2; fopt=active_coord_obj(topt,...)
       if fopt<beobj: beobj=fopt; beot=topt
       if beobj < active_coord_obj(x,...) - 1e-15:
          delta=beot-x
          if abs(delta)>1e-15:
             c_seg=c[i:i+n]  # length n view
             # But c_seg shares c; when we do c_seg += 2delta*p, p still old. Good.
             c_seg += 2.0*delta*p
             c_seg[i] += delta*delta
             p[i]=beot
             S += delta
             updates +=1
             if updates % 32 == 0:
                 pref,suff,top_idx=build_aux(c,top_count)
                 if order=='hot': maybe rebuild order? We can't rebuild order inside loop without messing. We can leave order; top_idx used for monitors. Hot order based old; okay.
    # normalize
    S=p.sum()
    if S < 1e-12: return uniform, conv, inf
    p=p/S
    c=conv_self(p)
    sc=score_array(p) # exact
    return p,c,sc
```
Need set `beobt` variable carefully. Use `beot=x`.
`active_coord_obj` with t hi maybe S_new negative? S_rest + t; if x=S and t=0, S_new=0 => inf. x likely <<S.
If `hi` very large and S_rest small, objective might prefer hi for denominator, but affected unmonitored. Okay.

Use active objective denominator S_new^2. If `out_max` stale but affected top current. Good.

### `ao_optimize` detailed code
```
def ao_optimize(p, budget_s, rng, inner=3, eta=0.4, temp=0.08, lam=0.3):
   p=normalize_p(p)
   n=len(p); M=fft_len(2*n-1); M2=fft_len(3*n-2)
   best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=score_array(p)
   t0=time.monotonic(); no=0
   while time.monotonic() < t0+budget_s:
      Pfft=np.fft.rfft(p,M); Prfft=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],M2)
      Q=p.copy(); zq=np.log(Q+1e-300); zq-=zq.max()
      for _ in range(inner):
         Qf=np.fft.rfft(Q,M)
         v=np.fft.irfft(Pfft*Qf,M)[:2*n-1].real
         v=np.clip(v,0,None); sv=v.sum()
         if sv<=0 or not finite: break
         v/=sv
         vmax=v.max(); vmin=v.min(); rngmax=max(1e-18,vmax-vmin)
         T=temp*rngmax
         scaled=(v-vmax)/T
         # clip scaled below -700
         w=np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -700, 0.0))
         sw=w.sum()
         if sw<=0 or not finite:
             w=np.zeros(2*n-1); w[int(np.argmax(v))]=1.0
         else: w/=sw
         Wf=np.fft.rfft(w,M2)
         h=np.fft.irfft(Prfft*Wf,M2)[n-1:2*n-1].real
         h=np.clip(h,0,None)
         hrel=n*h
         d=hrel-hrel.mean()
         dmax=np.max(np.abs(d))
         if dmax>1e-14:
             zq -= eta * (d/dmax)
      zq -= zq.mean(); zq -= zq.max()
      Q=np.exp(zq); Qs=Q.sum()
      if Qs<=0 or not finite: Q=p
      else: Q/=Qs
      p_new=(1.0-lam)*p + lam*Q
      p=normalize_p(p_new)
      sc=score_array(p)
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy(); no=0
          lam=min(0.6, lam*1.05)
      else:
          no += 1
          if no % 10 == 0:
             # perturb best or current? current p
             mode=int(rng.integers(0,3))
             p=perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.1+0.2*rng.random(), mode=mode)
             lam=max(0.1, lam*0.9)
      # maybe accept p if not too bad? Already p current.
   return p,best_p,best_sc
```
Could score_array every outer compute conv. Fine. `perturb_p` may return p with sum normalized.

### `mirror_optimize`
```
def mirror_optimize(p,budget_s,rng,alpha=0.15,temp=0.1):
   p=normalize_p(p); n=len(p)
   z=np.log(p+1e-300); z-=z.max()
   best=p; best_sc=score_array(p)
   t0=...
   it=0
   while time<t0+budget:
      p=softmax(z)
      c=conv_self(p)
      vmax=c.max(); vmin=c.min(); T=temp*max(1e-18,vmax-vmin)
      w=softmax(c/T)
      h=cross(w,p)
      d=n*h; d-=mean; dmax...
      z -= alpha*(d/dmax)
      z -= z.mean(); z -= z.max()
      # avoid collapse: if max(p) > (50/n) maybe blend z with log uniform? uniform log constant irrelevant, no. Could blend p with uniform:
      if p.max() > 20.0/n and it%10==0:
          p = 0.8*p + 0.2/n; p=normalize; z=log(p); z-=max
      sc=score_array(p) if it%5==0 else None
      if sc<best: update
      it++
   return p,best,best_sc
```
Softmax helper? We can define `softmax_z(z)` top-level.
```
def softmax_z(z):
   z=z-z.max(); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(len(z))/len(z); return e/s
```
`cross_weights_p(w,p)` returns h.
Use `alpha` maybe 0.2. This may be too aggressive; use alpha=0.1.
Add to optimize_length before AO: budget 15%.

### `estimate_betas`
```
def estimate_betas(p):
  c=conv_self(p); vals=c; cm=c.max(); mn=c.min(); rngv=cm-mn
  if not finite or rngv<1e-15: rngv=max(1e-15, cm*1e-3)
  betas=[]
  for fr in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
     b=1.0/(fr*rngv)
     if 1e2 < b < 1e7: betas.append(float(b))
  return betas
```
Maybe LSE objective with beta high causes weights active; if too high gradient sparse. We run several.

### `run_lse`
Inside `minimize`, if x length n-1, for n=1? not.
```
def run_lse(p,beta,maxfun=2000):
   p0=normalize_p(p); n=len(p)
   pf=p0+1e-300; pf=pf/pf.sum()
   den=max(float(pf[-1]),1e-300)
   x=np.log(pf) - np.log(den)
   x = np.clip(x,-50,50) maybe? Clipping might distort but prevent overflow. Use no clip? z max in objective handles. But x large 690 okay. L-BFGS steps maybe inf. Clip to [-100,100].
   grad_scale=1e6/max(1,n)
   try:
      res=minimize(lse_z_objective, x, args=(beta,n,grad_scale), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':int(max(10, maxfun//4)), 'maxfun':int(maxfun), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':20})
      x_new=res.x
   except Exception: x_new=x
   if not finite: x_new=x
   z=np.empty(n); z[:-1]=x_new; z[-1]=0.0; z-=z.max(); p_new=softmax_z(z); p_new=normalize_p(p_new)
   sc=score_array(p_new)
   if not finite: return p0,score_array(p0)
   return p_new,sc
```
`gtol` scaled; if grad scaled to ~1, gtol 1e-6 maybe. Use 1e-6.

`lse_z_objective`:
```
def lse_z_objective(x,beta,n,grad_scale):
    z=np.empty(n); z[:-1]=x; z[-1]=0.0
    z-=z.max()
    p=np.exp(z); s=p.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    p/=s
    c=conv_self(p)
    cm=float(np.max(c))
    scaled=beta*(c-cm)
    scaled=np.clip(scaled, -700, 0.0)
    ew=np.exp(scaled); sw=ew.sum()
    if sw<=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    LSE=cm + np.log(sw)/beta
    score=2.0*n*LSE
    if not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    w=ew/sw
    h=cross_weights_p(w,p)
    grad_p=2.0*h
    dot=float(np.dot(p,grad_p))
    gz=p*(grad_p-dot*p)
    gx=gz[:-1]*grad_scale
    return score,gx
```
Since p sum1, score=2n LSE exact; if p sum not exactly due normalize, use exact? LSE approximates max; scale invariant; okay.

### Potential issue: L-BFGS on n large with many parameters and objective flat along? Fixed last breaks scale. But if p last becomes 0, log init den floor; objective can increase it if beneficial. Fine.

### `perturb_p`
```
def perturb_p(p,rng,strength=0.1,mode=0):
   n=len(p); p=normalize_p(p)
   if mode==0:
      fmax=rng.uniform(2.0,8.0)
      freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
      damp=np.exp(-(freq/fmax)**2)
      f=np.fft.irfft(rng.standard_normal(n//2+1)*damp,n)
      sd=f.std(); if sd>1e-12: f=strength*f/sd
      z=np.log(p+1e-300)+f; return normalize_p(softmax_z(z))
   elif mode==1:
      alpha=min(0.5, strength*2)
      return normalize_p((1-alpha)*p + alpha/n)
   else:
      # random coordinate swap moves
      p2=p.copy(); c=conv_self(p2); k=int(np.argmax(c))
      d=np.zeros(n); j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1); d[j0:j1+1]=p2[j0:j1+1][::-1]
      donors=np.argpartition(d, -max(1,n//20))[-max(1,n//20):]
      recips=np.argpartition(d, max(1,n//20))[:max(1,n//20)]
      # move 10% from top donors to recipients
      tot=0; for i in donors: p2[i]=0; tot+=p2[i]? Need store.
```

```

d...
num=max(1,n//50)
donor_idx=np.argpartition(d, -num)[-num:]
recip_idx=np.argpartition(d, num)[:num]
moved=0
for i in donor_idx:
   amt=p2[i]*min(0.5, strength*3)
   p2[i]-=amt; moved+=amt
if moved>0 and len(recip_idx):
   weights=1.0/(d[recip_idx]+1e-12); weights/=weights.sum()
   p2[recip_idx] += moved*weights
```

```
def optimize_length(p, budget_s, rng, label=''):
```

```
beot=x; beobj=active_coord_obj(x,...)
f0=active_coord_obj(0,...); if f0<beobj: ...
fhi=active_coord_obj(hi,...); if fhi<beobj: ...
# only golden if hi>lo+...
```
If active base size 0 and out_max positive, obj decreasing with t (den larger), hi chosen; update t=hi likely. It may concentrate mass on coordinate; maybe bad but active unmonitored hidden peaks. Since no active affected means no top lags involving i, maybe increasing i could create new peaks not monitored. But golden chooses hi; could be bad. To avoid, if base.size==0, limit hi small: `hi = min(hi, max(0.001, x+10/n))`. Good. If no monitored affected, don't move large. Add:
```
if base.size == 0: hi = min(hi, max(1e-6, x + 10.0/n))
```

`c_seg = c[i:i+n]` length n; `c_seg += 2.0*delta*p` uses p old with p[i]=x. But p array is being modified in place; p[i] remains old until after. Good. `c_seg[i] += delta*delta`: Here c_seg is a view; after `c_seg += ...`, it has added 2delta*x at diag; then add delta². Good. Then p[i]=beot.
If delta negative, `c_seg += negative` may make small negative; no clip. At end recompute.

`S` may become negative due numerical? S is sum p; if p nonneg and t≥0, S≥0. If S<1e-12, return uniform.

`order_arr` for hot: `np.argsort(d)[::-1]` sorts full n; for n=12288 okay. If top_idx empty no hot. For random, `rng.permutation(n)` if rng not None.

### Mirror timestamp
`mirror_optimize` uses `score_array(p)` every 10 iterations; computing conv again. But we already have c; to compute score we can use cmax: `sc=2*n*c.max()/(S*S)` with S=p.sum(). Use current c to avoid another conv. Let's implement in mirror: after c, `sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))/(s*s)`. Then no extra. In AO, we compute score separately with conv(p,p) not reusing Q? We could compute p*p for score; okay.

For AO, after p update, `score_array(p)` computes conv. Could instead in next outer Pfft? no.

```
   m=z.max(); if not finite m: return ones

```

```
def optimize_length(p, budget_s, rng):
    p=normalize_p(p)
    best_p=p.copy()
    best_sc=score_array(p)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t0+max(5.0,float(budget_s))-2.0
    n=len(p)
    # mirror
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remaining>40:
       mb=min(120.0, 0.15*float(budget_s), remaining*0.3)
       if mb>10:
           p_m,best_m,sc_m=mirror_optimize(best_p,mb,rng)
           if sc_m<best_sc: ...
    # AO
    remaining=deadline-now
    if remaining>80:
       ab=min(420.0, 0.30*float(budget_s), remaining*0.4)
       if ab>30: p_a,best_a,sc_a=ao_optimize(best_p,ab,rng); update
    # LSE
    remaining=deadline-now
    if remaining>120 and minimize is not None:
       betas=estimate_betas(best_p)
       run_count=0
       for beta in betas[:2]:
          remaining=...
          if remaining<60: break
          maxfun=500 if n>10000 else 1200
          p_l,sc_l=run_lse(best_p,beta,maxfun)
          update
          run_count+=1
          # short CD after lse
          remaining=...
          if remaining>40:
             pc=best_p.copy(); cc=conv_self(pc); sc_c=score_array(pc)
             topc=60 if n>10000 else 100
             for sw in range(2):
                remaining=...
                if remaining<20: break
                sc_order='hot' if sw==0 else 'random'
                pc,cc,sc_c=active_cd_sweep(pc,cc,top_count=topc,order=order,rng=rng)
                if sc_c<best_sc: ...
    # AO2
    remaining=deadline-now
    if remaining>100:
       ab=min(300.0, 0.20*float(budget_s), remaining*0.5); update
    # CD main
    remaining=deadline-now
    if remaining>80:
       p_cur=best_p.copy(); c_cur=conv_self(p_cur); sc_cur=score_array(p_cur)
       top_count=80 if n>10000 else 120
       sweep=0; no=0
       while remaining>40:
           if sweep % 3 == 0: order='hot'
           else: order='random' if rng is not None else 'seq'
           p_cur,c_cur,sc_cur=active_cd_sweep(p_cur,c_cur,top_count,top_count,order,rng)
           if sc_cur<best_sc: best... no=0
           else:
              no += 1
              if sc_cur > best_sc*1.05:
                 p_cur=best_p.copy(); c_cur=conv_self(p_cur); sc_cur=best_sc; no += 2
           if no>=8:
              p_cur=perturb_p(best_p,rng,strength=0.1+0.3*rng.random(),mode=int(rng.integers(0,3)))
              c_cur=conv_self(p_cur); sc_cur=score_array(p_cur)
              no=0
           sweep += 1
           remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
    return best_p,best_sc
```

### `process_length` top seeds
After selecting top, we should not rescore top again? okay.
```
def process_length(N,budget,parent,rng,extra):
    seeds=make_seeds(parent,N,rng)
    if extra: seeds.extend(extra)
    top=select_best_seeds(seeds,3)
    if not top:
       p=np.ones(N)/N; s=score_array(p); return p,s
    best_p,best_sc=top[0][0],top[0][1]
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(5,budget)
    # allocate: first seed 55%, second 25%, third 20% of budget but respecting remaining
    fracs=[0.55,0.25,0.20]
    for idx in range(min(3,len(top))):
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if remaining<30: break
       b=min(budget*fracs[idx], remaining-5.0)
       if b<20: continue
       p_new,sc_new=optimize_length(top[idx][0],b,rng)
       if sc_new<best_sc: best_p=p_new.copy(); best_sc=sc_new
    return best_p,best_sc
```
If first seed optimized and improves, second starts from its own seed, not from best. That provides multi-start. Good.

```
 seeds=[]

   seeds.append(perturb_p(p,rng,0.15,0))
   seeds.append(perturb_p(p,rng,0.3,2))
 # uniform
 seeds.append(np.ones(N)/N)
 # ramps
 for a in [0.5,1.0,1.5,-0.5,-1.0,-1.5]:

 # cosines

 # random smooth/poly 6
```

`cosine_seed` with odd:
```
def cosine_seed(n,m,alpha,phase=0.0,odd=False):
    x=np.arange(n)/(n-1 if n>1 else 1)
    if odd:
       ang=(2*m+1)*np.pi*x + phase
    else:
       ang=2*np.pi*m*x + phase
    p=1.0+alpha*np.cos(ang)
    p=np.clip(p,1e-10,None)
    return normalize_p(p)
```
For alpha=1 and odd, p at x=1: 1+cos(oddπ)=0; okay clip.

```
   weights=rng.exponential(size=B)
```

```
def polynomial_seed(n,rng,degree=3,max_coeff=1.2):
   x=np.linspace(-0.5,0.5,n)
   p=np.ones(n)
   for k in range(1,degree+1):
      c=rng.uniform(-max_coeff,max_coeff)
      p += c*(x**k)
   p=np.clip(p,1e-10,None)
   return normalize_p(p)
```

### `resample_sequence`
```
def resample_sequence(old,n):
   old=np.asarray(old,float).ravel(); old=np.where(finite,0); old=np.clip(old,0,None)
   if old.size==0: return np.ones(n)/n
   if old.size==n: return normalize_p(old)
   if old.size==1: return np.full(n, old[0]/n)
   xo=np.arange(old.size, dtype=float)
   xn=np.linspace(xo[0], xo[-1], n)
   p=np.interp(xn, xo, old)
   p=np.clip(p,0,None)
   return normalize_p(p)
```

Let's write code now.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a, full))/(sum(a))^2.

The program uses several complementary heuristics:
  * coarse-to-fine seed generation,
  * mirror-descent on the softmax parametrization with smoothed max,
  * an alternating min-max game (multiplicative weights) for one LM-light
    linear-program update,
  * L-BFGS on a smoothed LSE objective in logit coordinates,
  * an active-set coordinate sweep for local polishing.

The code is designed to run safely under a 2400 second wall-clock budget
and to return the best sequence found.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


def fft_len(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -1.0e3)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z.copy()
    m = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones_like(z) / float(z.size)
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(z) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def normalize_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        n = p.size if p.size > 0 else 64
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return p / s


def conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    M = fft_len(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:m]
    c = np.real(c)
    return np.clip(c, 0.0, None)


def cross_weights_p(w, p):
    """
    Returns h[j] = sum_k w[k] * p[k-j] for valid k, j.
    w has length 2n-1, p has length n.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    rev = p[::-1]
    L = 3 * n - 2
    M = fft_len(L)
    PR = np.fft.rfft(rev, M)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    out = np.fft.irfft(PR * W, M)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.real(out)
    return np.clip(out, 0.0, None)


def score_array(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_self(p)
    cm = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return float("inf")
    val = 2.0 * p.size * cm / (s * s)
    return float(val) if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def resample_sequence(old, n):
    old = np.asarray(old, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old = np.where(np.isfinite(old), old, 0.0)
    old = np.clip(old, 0.0, None)
    if old.size == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if old.size == int(n):
        return normalize_p(old)
    if old.size == 1:
        v = float(old[0])
        if v <= 0.0:
            return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return np.full(int(n), v / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    xo = np.arange(old.size, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = np.linspace(xo[0], xo[-1], int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.interp(xn, xo, old)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    return normalize_p(p)


def random_smooth_p(n, rng, alpha=0.8, fmax=3.0):
    n = int(n)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    damp = np.exp(-(freqs / float(max(1.0, fmax))) ** 2)
    coeff = rng.standard_normal(n // 2 + 1) * damp
    f = np.fft.irfft(coeff, n)
    sd = float(f.std())
    if sd > 1e-12 and np.isfinite(sd):
        f = alpha * f / sd
    else:
        f = alpha * f
    p = np.exp(np.clip(f, -50.0, 50.0))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    return normalize_p(p)


def cosine_seed(n, m, alpha, phase=0.0, odd=False):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    if odd:
        th = (2 * int(m) + 1) * np.pi * x + float(phase)
    else:
        th = 2.0 * np.pi * int(m) * x + float(phase)
    p = 1.0 + float(alpha) * np.cos(th)
    p = np.clip(p, 1e-10, None)
    return normalize_p(p)


def polynomial_seed(n, rng, degree=3, max_coeff=1.2):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, int(degree) + 1):
        c = rng.uniform(-float(max_coeff), float(max_coeff))
        p = p + c * (x ** k)
    p = np.clip(p, 1e-10, None)
    return normalize_p(p)


def block_seed(n, rng, B):
    n = int(n)
    B = int(max(2, B))
    B = int(min(B, n))
    starts = np.linspace(0.0, float(n), B + 1, dtype=np.float64).astype(np.int64)
    starts[-1] = n
    weights = rng.exponential(size=B)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(B):
        sl = slice(int(starts[i]), int(starts[i + 1]))
        p[sl] = float(weights[i])
    return normalize_p(p)


def perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.15, mode=0):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n = p.size
    mode = int(mode)
    if mode == 0:
        fmax = rng.uniform(2.0, 8.0)
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
        damp = np.exp(-(freqs / float(max(1.0, fmax))) ** 2)
        coeff = rng.standard_normal(n // 2 + 1) * damp
        f = np.fft.irfft(coeff, n)
        sd = float(f.std())
        if sd > 1e-12 and np.isfinite(sd):
            f = float(strength) * f / sd
        else:
            f = float(strength) * f
        z = np.log(p + 1e-300) + f
        return normalize_p(softmax_z(z))
    elif mode == 1:
        alpha = float(min(0.5, max(0.01, strength * 2.0)))
        q = (1.0 - alpha) * p + alpha * (1.0 / float(n))
        return normalize_p(q)
    else:
        q = p.copy()
        c = conv_self(q)
        k = int(np.argmax(c))
        d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        j1 = min(k, n - 1)
        d[j0:j1 + 1] = q[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]
        num = max(1, min(n, n // 50))
        donor = np.argpartition(d, -num)[-num:]
        recip = np.argpartition(d, num)[:num]
        moved = 0.0
        fr = float(min(0.6, max(0.05, strength * 3.0)))
        for idx in donor:
            idx = int(idx)
            amt = q[idx] * fr
            q[idx] -= amt
            moved += amt
        if moved > 1e-15 and recip.size > 0:
            wts = 1.0 / (d[recip] + 1e-12)
            wts = np.maximum(wts, 0.0)
            sw = float(wts.sum())
            if sw > 0.0:
                q[recip] += moved * (wts / sw)
        return normalize_p(q)


def make_seeds(parent, n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    seeds = []

    if parent is not None:
        p0 = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        seeds.append(p0)
        seeds.append(perturb_p(p0, rng, strength=0.15, mode=0))
        seeds.append(perturb_p(p0, rng, strength=0.25, mode=2))

    seeds.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    # Linear ramps, both directions.
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        x = x / float(n - 1)
    else:
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for a in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, -0.5, -1.0, -1.5):
        v = 1.0 + a * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
        v = np.clip(v, 1e-10, None)
        seeds.append(normalize_p(v))

    # Cosine / odd-cosine seeds.
    for m in (1, 2, 3, 5):
        for odd in (0, 1):
            for alpha in (0.5, 1.0):
                seeds.append(cosine_seed(n, m, alpha, 0.0, odd=bool(odd)))

    # Smooth random / polynomial seeds.
    for _ in range(3):
        seeds.append(random_smooth_p(n, rng, alpha=0.8, fmax=3.0))
    for _ in range(3):
        seeds.append(polynomial_seed(n, rng, degree=3, max_coeff=1.2))

    # Blocky seed.
    B = max(6, n // 32)
    seeds.append(block_seed(n, rng, B))

    return seeds


def select_best_seeds(seeds, topk=3):
    scored = []
    for p in seeds:
        p = normalize_p(p)
        s = score_array(p)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((p, float(s)))
    if not scored:
        return []
    arr = np.array([s for _, s in scored], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(arr)
    out = []
    for idx in order:
        if len(out) >= int(topk):
            break
        p, s = scored[int(idx)]
        out.append((p, float(s)))
    return out


def build_aux(c, top_count):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    top_count = int(min(max(1, top_count), L))
    order = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_count]
    pref = np.maximum.accumulate(c)
    suff = np.maximum.accumulate(c[::-1])[::-1]
    return pref, suff, order


def active_coord_obj(t, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diag):
    Snew = S_rest + float(t)
    den = Snew * Snew
    if den <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(den):
        return 1.0e100
    mx = float(out_max)
    if base is not None and base.size > 0:
        vals = base + 2.0 * t * A + (t * t) * diag
        v = float(np.max(vals))
        if v > mx:
            mx = v
    val = mx / den
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1.0e100
    return float(val)


def active_cd_sweep(p, c, top_count=120, order="random", rng=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        p = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
        c = conv_self(p)
        return p, c, score_array(p)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        S = 1.0
        c = conv_self(p)

    pref, suff, top_idx = build_aux(c, top_count)

    if order == "hot" and top_idx.size > 0:
        k = int(top_idx[0])
        d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        j1 = min(k, n - 1)
        d[j0:j1 + 1] = p[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]
        order_arr = np.argsort(d)[::-1]
    elif order == "seq":
        order_arr = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        if rng is None:
            order_arr = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            order_arr = rng.permutation(n)

    updates = 0
    gr = 0.3819660112501051

    for ii in order_arr:
        i = int(ii)
        x = float(p[i])

        out_max = 0.0
        if i > 0:
            out_max = max(out_max, float(pref[i - 1]))
        endk = i + n
        if endk < L:
            out_max = max(out_max, float(suff[endk]))

        ks = top_idx
        if ks.size > 0:
            aff = (ks >= i) & (ks < i + n)
            if np.any(~aff):
                om = float(np.max(c[ks[~aff]]))
                if om > out_max:
                    out_max = om
            if np.any(aff):
                ks_aff = ks[aff]
                j = ks_aff - i
                old = c[ks_aff]
                p_aff = p[j]
                diag = (j == i)
                base = old - 2.0 * x * p_aff
                if np.any(diag):
                    base = base.copy()
                    base[diag] = base[diag] + x * x
                A = p_aff.copy()
                if np.any(diag):
                    A[diag] = 0.0
                diagf = diag.astype(np.float64)
            else:
                base = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
                A = base
                diagf = base
        else:
            base = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
            A = base
            diagf = base

        S_rest = S - x
        hi = float(max(0.005, 3.0 * x + 1.0 / float(n), 50.0 / float(n)))
        hi = float(min(1.0, hi))
        if hi < x:
            hi = float(min(1.0, x + 1e-6))
        if hi <= 0.0:
            hi = 1e-8
        if base.size == 0:
            hi = min(hi, max(1e-6, x + 10.0 / float(n)))

        beot = x
        beobj = active_coord_obj(x, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diagf)

        f0 = active_coord_obj(0.0, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diagf)
        if f0 < beobj:
            beobj = f0
            beot = 0.0

        fhi = active_coord_obj(hi, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diagf)
        if fhi < beobj:
            beobj = fhi
            beot = hi

        if hi > 1e-12:
            lo = 0.0
            u = lo + (1.0 - gr) * (hi - lo)
            v = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
            fu = active_coord_obj(u, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diagf)
            fv = active_coord_obj(v, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diagf)
            for _ in range(22):
                if fu < fv:
                    hi = v
                    v = u
                    fv = fu
                    u = lo + (1.0 - gr) * (hi - lo)
                    fu = active_coord_obj(u, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diagf)
                else:
                    lo = u
                    u = v
                    fu = fv
                    v = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
                    fv = active_coord_obj(v, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diagf)
            topt = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            fopt = active_coord_obj(topt, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diagf)
            if fopt < beobj:
                beobj = fopt
                beot = topt

        cur_obj = active_coord_obj(x, S_rest, out_max, base, A, diagf)
        if beobj < cur_obj - 1e-15:
            delta = float(beot) - x
            if abs(delta) > 1e-15:
                seg = c[i:i + n]
                seg = seg + 2.0 * delta * p
                seg[i] = seg[i] + delta * delta
                c[i:i + n] = seg
                p[i] = float(beot)
                S += delta
                updates += 1
                if updates % 32 == 0:
                    pref, suff, top_idx = build_aux(c, top_count)
                    if S <= 1e-9:
                        break

    S2 = float(p.sum())
    if S2 <= 1e-12:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        c = conv_self(p)
        return p, c, score_array(p)

    p = p / S2
    c = conv_self(p)
    sc = score_array(p)
    return p, c, sc


def mirror_optimize(p, budget_s, rng, alpha=0.12, temp=0.10):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n = p.size
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_array(p)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, float(budget_s)) - 1.0

    z = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    z -= float(z.max())

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = softmax_z(z)
        p = normalize_p(p)
        c = conv_self(p)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            break
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        cn = float(np.min(c))
        rngv = max(1e-18, cm - cn)
        T = float(temp) * rngv
        scaled = (c - cm) / max(1e-300, T)
        ew = np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0))
        sw = float(ew.sum())
        if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = ew / sw
        h = cross_weights_p(w, p)
        hrel = float(n) * h
        d = hrel - float(np.mean(hrel))
        dm = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if dm > 1e-14:
            z = z - float(alpha) * (d / dm)
            z -= float(z.mean())
            z -= float(z.max())

        if it % 8 == 0:
            sc = 2.0 * n * cm / (s * s)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_p = p.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                alpha = min(0.30, alpha * 1.03)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= 25:
                    z = np.log(perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.15, mode=int(rng.integers(0, 3))) + 1e-300)
                    z -= float(z.max())
                    no_improve = 0
                    alpha = max(0.04, alpha * 0.85)

        # Avoid too much mass collapse by occasional weak mixing with uniform.
        if it % 50 == 0 and float(np.max(p)) > 35.0 / n:
            p = 0.85 * p + 0.15 * (1.0 / float(n))
            p = normalize_p(p)
            z = np.log(p + 1e-300)
            z -= float(z.max())

        it += 1

    if float(time.monotonic() - t0) > 0:
        return p, best_p, best_sc
    return p, best_p, best_sc


def ao_optimize(p, budget_s, rng, inner=3, eta=0.35, temp=0.08, lam=0.30):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n = p.size
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_array(p)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, float(budget_s)) - 1.0

    M = fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    M2 = fft_len(3 * n - 2)

    no_improve = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        Pfft = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
        Prfft = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M2)

        Q = p.copy()
        zq = np.log(Q + 1e-300)
        zq -= float(zq.max())

        for _ in range(int(inner)):
            Qf = np.fft.rfft(Q, M)
            v = np.fft.irfft(Pfft * Qf, M)[:2 * n - 1].real
            v = np.real(v)
            v = np.clip(v, 0.0, None)
            sv = float(v.sum())
            if sv <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(sv):
                break
            v = v / sv
            vmax = float(np.max(v))
            vmin = float(np.min(v))
            dng = max(1e-18, vmax - vmin)
            T = float(temp) * dng
            scaled = (v - vmax) / max(1e-300, T)
            ew = np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0))
            sw = float(ew.sum())
            if sw <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sw):
                w = np.zeros_like(v)
                w[int(np.argmax(v))] = 1.0
            else:
                w = ew / sw

            Wf = np.fft.rfft(w, M2)
            h = np.fft.irfft(Prfft * Wf, M2)[n - 1:2 * n - 1].real
            h = np.clip(np.real(h), 0.0, None)
            hrel = float(n) * h
            d = hrel - float(np.mean(hrel))
            dm = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if dm > 1e-14:
                zq = zq - float(eta) * (d / dm)

        zq -= float(zq.mean())
        zq -= float(zq.max())
        Q = softmax_z(zq)
        Q = normalize_p(Q)

        p = normalize_p((1.0 - float(lam)) * p + float(lam) * Q)
        sc = score_array(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            lam = min(0.60, lam * 1.05)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve == 15:
                p = perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.15, mode=int(rng.integers(0, 3)))
                lam = max(0.10, lam * 0.90)
            elif no_improve == 30:
                p = best_p.copy()
                lam = max(0.20, lam * 0.80)
                no_improve = 0

    return p, best_p, best_sc


def estimate_betas(p):
    c = conv_self(p)
    cm = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    cn = float(np.min(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    rngv = cm - cn
    if not np.isfinite(rngv) or rngv < 1e-15:
        rngv = max(1e-15, abs(cm) * 1e-3)
    betas = []
    for fr in (0.20, 0.10, 0.05, 0.025, 0.0125):
        b = 1.0 / (float(fr) * rngv)
        if 1e2 < b < 1e7 and np.isfinite(b):
            betas.append(float(b))
    return betas


def lse_z_objective(x, beta, n, grad_scale):
    z = np.empty(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    z[:-1] = x
    z[-1] = 0.0
    z -= float(z.max())
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(max(1, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s

    c = conv_self(p)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    scaled = float(beta) * (c - cm)
    ew = np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0))
    sw = float(ew.sum())
    if sw <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(max(1, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    LSE = cm + float(np.log(sw)) / float(beta)
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * LSE
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(max(1, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    w = ew / sw
    h = cross_weights_p(w, p)
    grad_p = 2.0 * h
    dot = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    gz = p * (grad_p - dot * p)
    gx = gz[:-1] * float(grad_scale)
    gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
    return float(score), gx


def run_lse(p, beta, maxfun=1200):
    p0 = normalize_p(p)
    n = p0.size
    if minimize is None:
        return p0, score_array(p0)

    pf = p0 + 1e-300
    pf = pf / float(pf.sum())
    den = max(float(pf[-1]), 1e-300)
    x = np.log(pf) - float(np.log(den))
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -60.0)
    x = np.clip(x, -100.0, 100.0)
    grad_scale = 1.0e6 / float(max(1, n))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_z_objective,
            x,
            args=(float(beta), int(n), float(grad_scale)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(max(10, maxfun // 4)),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxls": 20,
            },
        )
        x_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        x_new = x

    if x_new.size != n - 1:
        x_new = x
    x_new = np.where(np.isfinite(x_new), x_new, 0.0)

    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[:-1] = x_new
    z[-1] = 0.0
    p_new = normalize_p(softmax_z(z))
    sc = score_array(p_new)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return p0, score_array(p0)
    return p_new, float(sc)


def optimize_length(p, budget_s, rng):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_array(p)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, float(budget_s)) - 2.0
    n = p.size

    # Phase 1: cheap mirror descent.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 40.0:
        mb = min(150.0, 0.15 * float(budget_s), 0.30 * rem)
        if mb > 10.0:
            _, bm, sm = mirror_optimize(best_p, mb, rng, alpha=0.12, temp=0.10)
            if np.isfinite(sm) and sm < best_sc:
                best_p = bm.copy()
                best_sc = float(sm)
                print(f"    mirror improved -> {best_sc:.10f}")

    # Phase 2: AO / multiplicative min-max.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        ab = min(500.0, 0.30 * float(budget_s), 0.40 * rem)
        if ab > 25.0:
            _, ba, sa = ao_optimize(best_p, ab, rng, inner=3, eta=0.32, temp=0.08, lam=0.30)
            if np.isfinite(sa) and sa < best_sc:
                best_p = ba.copy()
                best_sc = float(sa)
                print(f"    AO improved -> {best_sc:.10f}")

    # Phase 3: LSE / L-BFGS refinement.
    if minimize is not None:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 120.0:
            betas = estimate_betas(best_p)
            for beta in betas[:2]:
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 70.0:
                    break
                maxfun = 450 if n > 10000 else 1100
                pl, sl = run_lse(best_p, beta, maxfun)
                if np.isfinite(sl) and sl < best_sc:
                    best_p = pl.copy()
                    best_sc = float(sl)
                    print(f"    LSE beta={beta:.3e} improved -> {best_sc:.10f}")

                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 35.0:
                    pc = best_p.copy()
                    cc = conv_self(pc)
                    sc_cur = score_array(pc)
                    topc = 60 if n > 10000 else 100
                    for sw in range(2):
                        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                        if rem < 18.0:
                            break
                        od = "hot" if sw == 0 else "random"
                        pc, cc, sc_cur = active_cd_sweep(pc, cc, top_count=topc, order=od, rng=rng)
                        if np.isfinite(sc_cur) and sc_cur < best_sc:
                            best_p = pc.copy()
                            best_sc = float(sc_cur)
                            print(f"    CD after LSE improved -> {best_sc:.10f}")

    # Phase 4: second AO pass from best.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100.0:
        ab = min(350.0, 0.20 * float(budget_s), 0.50 * rem)
        if ab > 25.0:
            _, ba, sa = ao_optimize(best_p, ab, rng, inner=2, eta=0.25, temp=0.05, lam=0.25)
            if np.isfinite(sa) and sa < best_sc:
                best_p = ba.copy()
                best_sc = float(sa)
                print(f"    AO2 improved -> {best_sc:.10f}")

    # Phase 5: main active-set coordinate polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0:
        pc = best_p.copy()
        cc = conv_self(pc)
        sc_cur = score_array(pc)
        topc = 80 if n > 10000 else 120
        sweeps = 0
        no_improve = 0

        while rem > 35.0:
            if sweeps % 3 == 0:
                od = "hot"
            else:
                od = "random" if rng is not None else "seq"

            pc, cc, sc_cur = active_cd_sweep(pc, cc, top_count=topc, order=od, rng=rng)

            if np.isfinite(sc_cur) and sc_cur < best_sc:
                best_p = pc.copy()
                best_sc = float(sc_cur)
                no_improve = 0
                if sweeps <= 5 or sweeps % 5 == 0:
                    print(f"    CD sweep {sweeps} improved -> {best_sc:.10f}")
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if np.isfinite(sc_cur) and sc_cur > best_sc * 1.04:
                    pc = best_p.copy()
                    cc = conv_self(pc)
                    sc_cur = best_sc
                    no_improve += 2

            if no_improve >= 8:
                pc = perturb_p(best_p, rng, strength=0.10 + 0.25 * float(rng.random()),
                               mode=int(rng.integers(0, 3)))
                cc = conv_self(pc)
                sc_cur = score_array(pc)
                no_improve = 0

            sweeps += 1
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()

    # Phase 6: final small mirror/AO if time remains.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        mb = min(90.0, 0.30 * rem)
        _, bm, sm = mirror_optimize(best_p, mb, rng, alpha=0.08, temp=0.06)
        if np.isfinite(sm) and sm < best_sc:
            best_p = bm.copy()
            best_sc = float(sm)
            print(f"    final mirror improved -> {best_sc:.10f}")

    best_p = normalize_p(best_p)
    return best_p, score_array(best_p)


def process_length(n, budget, parent, rng, extra_seeds):
    n = int(n)
    seeds = make_seeds(parent, n, rng)
    if extra_seeds:
        seeds.extend(extra_seeds)

    top = select_best_seeds(seeds, 3)
    if not top:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return p, score_array(p)

    best_p, best_sc = top[0][0], top[0][1]
    print(f"  length={n} best seed score={best_sc:.10f}")

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget)) - 5.0
    fracs = (0.55, 0.25, 0.20)

    for idx in range(min(3, len(top))):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break
        b = float(budget) * fracs[idx]
        b = min(b, rem - 5.0)
        if b < 25.0:
            continue
        pn, sn = optimize_length(top[idx][0], b, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sn) and sn < best_sc:
            best_p = pn.copy()
            best_sc = float(sn)
            print(f"  length={n} refined seed #{idx} score={best_sc:.10f}")

    return best_p, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 45.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if hs is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.size > 64 and arr.sum() > 1e-12:
                parent = arr
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        pnorm = normalize_p(parent)
        sparent = score_array(pnorm)
        print(f"parent length={parent.size}, parent internal score={sparent:.10f}")
        if 256 <= parent.size <= 12288:
            n1 = int(parent.size)
        elif parent.size > 12288:
            n1 = 12288
        else:
            n1 = 8192
    else:
        n1 = 8192

    n2 = 12288 if n1 < 12288 else 8192
    print(f"chosen primary length={n1}, secondary length={n2}")

    extra = []

    # Coarse-to-fine seeds, only if plenty of time.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 180.0:
        coarse_specs = (
            (64, 18.0),
            (128, 25.0),
            (256, 30.0),
        )
        for nc, b in coarse_specs:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 100.0:
                break
            inits = []
            if parent is not None:
                inits.append(resample_sequence(parent, nc))
            inits.append(np.ones(nc, dtype=np.float64) / float(nc))
            inits.append(random_smooth_p(nc, rng, alpha=0.9, fmax=2.0))
            for ip in inits:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 70.0:
                    break
                try:
                    po, _ = optimize_length(ip, max(8.0, b), rng)
                    extra.append(resample_sequence(po, n1))
                except Exception:
                    pass

    print("Generating extra seeds: score candidates")
    valid_extra = []
    for p in extra:
        p = normalize_p(p)
        s = score_array(p)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            valid_extra.append(p)
    extra = valid_extra

    if n1 != n2 and deadline - time.monotonic() > 300.0:
        budget1 = 0.62 * (deadline - time.monotonic())
        budget2 = (deadline - time.monotonic()) - budget1 - 20.0
        if budget2 < 60.0:
            budget2 = max(30.0, 0.20 * (deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
            budget1 = (deadline - time.monotonic()) - budget2 - 20.0
    else:
        budget1 = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0)
        budget2 = 0.0

    best_p = np.ones(n1, dtype=np.float64) / float(n1)
    best_sc = score_array(best_p)

    if budget1 > 30.0:
        print(f"\n=== Primary length {n1}, budget {budget1:.1f}s ===")
        p1, s1 = process_length(n1, budget1, parent, rng, extra)
        if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_sc:
            best_p = p1.copy()
            best_sc = float(s1)
            print(f"primary best score={best_sc:.10f}")

    if budget2 > 30.0 and n1 != n2:
        print(f"\n=== Secondary length {n2}, budget {budget2:.1f}s ===")
        # Resample the current best to secondary length as a starting seed.
        extra2 = [resample_sequence(best_p, n2)]
        p2, s2 = process_length(n2, budget2, parent, rng, extra2)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_sc:
            best_p = p2.copy()
            best_sc = float(s2)
            print(f"secondary best score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Final short refinement on the global best with a small budget.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        fb = min(200.0, 0.55 * rem)
        print(f"\n=== Final refinement budget {fb:.1f}s ===")
        p_f, s_f = optimize_length(best_p, fb, rng)
        if np.isfinite(s_f) and s_f < best_sc:
            best_p = p_f.copy()
            best_sc = float(s_f)
            print(f"final refinement score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Very last perturb + polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 40.0:
        last = []
        for m in range(3):
            last.append(perturb_p(best_p, rng, strength=0.05 + 0.10 * float(rng.random()), mode=m))
        for lp in last:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 25.0:
                break
            p_x, s_x = optimize_length(lp, min(35.0, rem - 5.0), rng)
            if np.isfinite(s_x) and s_x < best_sc:
                best_p = p_x.copy()
                best_sc = float(s_x)

    best_p = normalize_p(best_p)
    best_sc = score_array(best_p)

    # Sanitize into a Python list of legal floats.
    out = []
    for v in best_p:
        v = float(v)
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            v = 0.0
        if v < 0.0:
            v = 0.0
        if v > 1000.0:
            v = 1000.0
        out.append(v)

    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    print(f"FINAL internal score={best_sc:.10f}, length={len(out)}")
    print(f"Time used={time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s / {total_budget:.1f}s")

    # Optional sanity check only for modest lengths and if enough time remains.
    try:
        if len(out) <= 10000 and time.monotonic() < t_start + total_budget - 8.0:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            print(f"Verified score from provided evaluator: {verified:.10f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out
```
